## 10. Next check

The premark TV seam is exhausted as a source-attached raw block by
(7.127at)--(7.127bd), and the existing common-prefix fork cannot type its
diffuse Quit occupation as chronology.  The signed cap displacements are now
proved summable along every fixed-fork positive-minimum replay, and the target
caps form a forward summable-residual Nash--Bellman chain whose finite
intervals reverse into chronological blocks.  The connector itself is now
exhausted by (7.127bv-q).  The next exact question is whether the root arm's
fixed actual absorption can be Nashified with projective residual small
relative to that same charge **without changing its source prefix**, or
whether the connector arm's definite spectator debt can be returned to the
same prefix rather than merely generating another paid port.  Any proposed
adapter must retain the original source prefix, the positive `D_*` survival
floor, and the full behavioral fork gain.  It must not invoke the
two-persistent residual consumer, since (7.127bv-g) proves its hypothesis
false on this ray.  Re-selecting another paid row or using the positive Never
cylinder as though it were a finite root does not address either typed gap.

The recursive version is exhausted by (7.127bv-t): exactification spends
off-minimum debt, while the next horizontal fork can replenish it through the
same unsigned spectator-transfer term.  Accordingly this local lane should
not be refined again.  The remaining research target is one nonlocal theorem
which types or charges a horizontal reset jointly with its following exact
cap segment, thereby controlling the cumulative `R_m` term.  Without such a
theorem the construction rigorously returns to the paid-port/source-reentry
waist.

Proposition 7.27.2 now makes the corresponding capacity question exact.  A
successful positive-minimum producer must construct, in one declared order,
an exact Nash--Bellman connector from one paid child's exit to the next
child's entry (or prove tail-insensitivity of the earlier child), while
preserving literal common-parent ancestry.  Separate exactness, common source,
common exit, reach, and charge are provably insufficient.  Once that connector
is produced, Proposition 7.26.2 supplies the shared finite capacity account;
without it, each regenerated child legitimately starts a new account.

Section 7.29 exhausts the proposed local use of punishment normality,
singleton margins, and the retained atom.  It recovers only named paid-port,
role-swapped externality, floor-safe exact-port, and nonrenewable killed-debt
outputs.  The next check must therefore be nonlocal: either construct the
same-parent connector required by Question 7.27.1 from an additional
hard-residual invariant, or give a shared cumulative account for the fixed
signed labels in Corollary 7.28.2.  Further first-disagreement localization
of one seam would duplicate the declarations listed in Section 7.29.

Section 7.30 also exhausts the finite minimax/occupation attack on the four
full responses.  The next genuinely global question is whether positive
minimum makes the Pareto or balanced correlated endpoint **product
realizable with controlled unrestricted caps**, or instead forces a terminal
row violation of every positive response normal to lower actual total debt.
Without one of those two carrier comparisons, the response hull supplies no
terminal Nash profile and no UE certificate.

Section 7.31 rules out the first interpretation if “product realizable” means
only a public phase lottery or an unguarded deterministic calendar.  The next
question is now narrower: can the hard source produce an exact semantic
collar connector which leaves `[U,B]`, returns to the same source genealogy,
and closes a finite guarded Nash--Bellman cycle of positive total absorption?
Without that connector, public mixing preserves at least `D_*` aggregate
debt and native private mixing changes the endpoint family through cross
vertices.

Section 7.32 also excludes an `O(h)` chattering/root-re-equilibration
substitute for the connector: its aggregate root error is bounded below by a
fixed multiple of total absorption, and its periodic unrestricted cap defect
has a positive limit.  Any surviving construction must therefore make an
order-one continuation excursion to a singleton-tie face and return with
literal Bellman/source ancestry.  The next check is whether the hard carrier
comparison forces such an excursion to cross an already consumed terminal or
off-minimum boundary, or whether it can return through a new global semantic
collar cycle.
